A Table of Generating Functions Thanks:
Robert Reynolds

Abstract
. This is a compendium of generating functions involving single, double sums and definite integrals. These generating functions also involve special functions in both the summand function and closed form solution.



2020 Mathematics Subject Classification
Primary 30E20, 33-01, 33-03, 33-04

1.
Preliminaries

1.1.
Lagrange Series

Theorem 1.1
.

The Lagrange series has two basic forms;

(1.1)

f(x)=f(0)+\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{y^{n}}{n!}\left[D^{n-1}\left(f^{\prime}(x)\right)\phi^{n}(x)\right]_{x=0}

and

(1.2)

\frac{f(x)}{1-y\phi^{\prime}(x)}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{y^{n}}{n!}\left[D^{n}\left(f(x)\right)\phi^{n}(x)\right]_{x=0},

where
y=x/\phi^{\prime}(x),D=d/dx
, and the primes denote derivatives.

In this compendium we apply Theorem (1.1) given by equation (18) in
[

]
to derive the propositions that follow. We apply the method in section (7) in

2.
Introduction

Generating functions are useful in various branches of mathematics, including combinatorics, number theory, and analysis. They offer a versatile and effective way to encode and manipulate sequences, leading to valuable insights and solutions to complex problems. One of the primary applications of generating functions is in counting and combinatorics. By representing combinatorial structures as generating functions, we can determine the number of objects or arrangements with specific properties by examining the coefficients of the generating function. Generating functions also come in handy when dealing with recurrence relations. By transforming a recurrence relation into an equation involving generating functions, we can manipulate the generating function algebraically to find a closed-form expression for the sequence. This approach simplifies the solution of recursive problems. In the area of partition theory, generating functions have proven their worth. They provide efficient computations of partition functions and offer insights into the properties of integer partitions. By analyzing the generating functions, we can gain a deeper understanding of the behaviour of partitions.

Generating functions are also valuable for asymptotic analysis. By studying the singularities of generating functions in the complex plane, we can determine growth rates, extract coefficients, and derive asymptotic estimates. This analysis helps us understand the long-term behaviour of sequences. Probability theory benefits from generating functions as well. Moment generating functions and probability generating functions encode information about the distribution of random variables. These functions offer insights into the statistical properties and moments of the variables. Readers interested in literature on generating functions can peruse the following works
[
4
,
5
,
6
,
7
,
1
,
3
,
8
,
22
,
23
]
.

The development of standard tables for binomial coefficient summations and the use of the Lagrange inversion theorem and contour integration contribute to the expansion of generating function techniques and their application in solving functional equations involving special functions.

3.
Generating functions involving the Hypergeometric functions

3.1.
Entry 1

Proposition 3.1
.

(3.1)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(n\beta+1)\left(x(x+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,-\alpha;n(\beta-1)+1;z)}{n!\Gamma(\beta n-n+1)}\\
=\frac{(x+1)(xz+1)^{\alpha}}{-\beta x+x+1}

where
f(x)=(x+1)(xz+1)^{\alpha}
and
\phi(x)=(x+1)^{\beta}
.

Example 3.2
.

(3.2)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(n\beta+1)\left(x(x+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,-\alpha;n(\beta-1)+1;z)}{\Gamma(n+2)\Gamma(\beta n-n+1)}\\
=\frac{(x+1)(xz+1)^{\alpha+1}\,_{2}F_{1}\left(1,\alpha-\beta+2;\alpha+2;\frac{xz+1}{1-z}\right)}{(\alpha+1)x(z-1)}\\
-\frac{(x+1)^{\beta}\,_{2}F_{1}\left(1,\alpha-\beta+2;\alpha+2;\frac{1}{1-z}\right)}{(\alpha+1)x(z-1)}

Example 3.3
.

(3.3)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(n\beta+1)\left(x(x+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,-\alpha;n(\beta-1)+1;z)}{(n+2)!\Gamma(\beta n-n+1)}\\
=\int_{0}^{x}\frac{\Gamma(\alpha+1)(s+1)^{-2\beta}((\beta-1)s-1)(x+1)^{2\beta}}{x^{2}(z-1)}\\
\left(\frac{(s+1)^{\beta-1}\,_{2}F_{1}\left(1,\alpha-\beta+2;\alpha+2;\frac{1}{1-z}\right)}{\Gamma(\alpha+2)}\right.\\
\left.-\frac{(sz+1)^{\alpha+1}\,_{2}F_{1}\left(1,\alpha-\beta+2;\alpha+2;\frac{sz+1}{1-z}\right)}{\Gamma(\alpha+2)}\right)ds

3.2.
Entry 2

Proposition 3.4
.

(3.4)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(a+1)\left(x(cx+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,-n\beta;a-n+1;c)}{n!\Gamma(a-n+1)}=-\frac{(x+1)^{a}(cx+1)}{\beta cx-cx-1}

where
f(x)=(x+1)^{a}(cx+1)
and
\phi(x)=(cx+1)^{\beta}
.

Example 3.5
.

(3.5)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(a+1)\left(x(xz+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,-n\beta;a-n+1;z)}{\Gamma(n+2)\Gamma(a-n+1)}\\
=\frac{(xz+1)^{\beta}\,_{2}F_{1}\left(1,a-\beta+2;a+2;\frac{z}{z-1}\right)}{(a+1)x(z-1)}\\
-\frac{(x+1)^{a+1}(xz+1)\,_{2}F_{1}\left(1,a-\beta+2;a+2;\frac{(x+1)z}{z-1}\right)}{(a+1)x(z-1)}

3.3.
Entry 3

Proposition 3.6
.

(3.6)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(n\beta+1)\left(x(x+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\,_{1}F_{1}(-n;\beta n-n+1;-z)}{n!\Gamma(\beta n-n+1)}=-\frac{(x+1)e^{xz}}{\beta x-x-1}

where
f(x)=e^{xz}(x+1)
and
\phi(x)=(x+1)^{\beta}
.

Example 3.7
.

(3.7)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(n\beta+1)\left(x(x+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\,_{1}F_{1}(-n;n(\beta-1)+1;-z)}{\Gamma(n+2)\Gamma(\beta n-n+1)}\\
=\frac{e^{-z}(x+1)^{\beta}(-z)^{\beta-1}\Gamma(1-\beta,-z)}{x}\\
-e^{-z}(-((x+1)z))^{\beta-1}\Gamma(1-\beta,-((x+1)z))\\
-\frac{e^{-z}(-((x+1)z))^{\beta-1}\Gamma(1-\beta,-((x+1)z))}{x}

3.4.
Entry 4

Proposition 3.8
.

(3.8)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(m+n+1)\left(x(x+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\Gamma((m+n)\beta+1)}{\Gamma(n+1)(m+n)!\Gamma(-n+(m+n)\beta+1)}\\
\,{}_{2}F_{1}(-n,-\alpha;m+(m+n)(\beta-1)+1;z)\\
=\frac{(x+1)^{\beta m+1}(xz+1)^{\alpha}}{(1-\beta)x+1}

where
f(x)=(x+1)x^{m}(xz+1)^{\alpha}
and
\phi(x)=(x+1)^{\beta}
.

Example 3.9
.

(3.9)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(m+n+1)\left(x(x+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\Gamma((m+n)\beta+1)}{\Gamma(n+1)(m+n+1)!\Gamma(-n+(m+n)\beta+1)}\\
\,{}_{2}F_{1}(-n,-\alpha;m+(m+n)(\beta-1)+1;z)\\
=\frac{(x+1)^{\beta+\beta m}F_{1}(m+1;\beta,-\alpha;m+2;-x,-xz)}{m+1}

3.5.
Entry 5.

Proposition 3.10
.

(3.10)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n}\left(x(1-x)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\Gamma((m+n)\beta+1)\,_{1}F_{1}(-n;m+(m+n)(\beta-1)+1;z)}{\Gamma(n+1)\Gamma(-n+(m+n)\beta+1)}\\
=\frac{(-1)^{-m}(x-1)e^{xz}(1-x)^{\beta m}}{(\beta-1)x+1}

where
f(x)=(x-1)x^{m}e^{xz}
and
\phi(x)=(1-x)^{\beta}
.

Example 3.11
.

(3.11)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n}\left(x(1-x)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\Gamma((m+n)\beta+1)\,_{1}F_{1}(-n;m+(m+n)(\beta-1)+1;z)}{(m+n+1)\Gamma(n+1)\Gamma(-n+(m+n)\beta+1)}\\
=-\int_{0}^{x}\frac{(-1)^{-m}s^{m}(1-s)^{-\beta}e^{sz}(1-x)^{\beta}\left(x(1-x)^{-\beta}\right)^{-m}}{x}ds

3.6.
Entry 6.

Proposition 3.12
.

(3.12)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(m+1)\left(xe^{-bx}\right)^{n}\,_{1}F_{1}(-n;m-n+1;-a-bn)}{n!\Gamma(m-n+1)}=\frac{e^{ax}(x+1)^{m}}{1-bx}

where
f(x)=(x+1)^{m}e^{x(a+b)}
and
\phi(x)=1
.

Example 3.13
.

(3.13)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(m+1)\left(xe^{-bx}\right)^{n}\,_{1}F_{1}(-n;m-n+1;-a-bn)}{\Gamma(n+2)\Gamma(m-n+1)}\\
=\frac{(b-a)^{-m-1}e^{-a+bx+b}\Gamma(m+1,b-a)}{x}\\
-\frac{(x+1)^{m+1}e^{-a+bx+b}(-((x+1)(a-b)))^{-m-1}\Gamma(m+1,-((a-b)(x+1)))}{x}

3.7.
Entry 7.

Proposition 3.14
.

(3.14)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n}\Gamma(\alpha+1)\left(x(xz+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,-((m+n)\beta);-n+\alpha+1;-z)}{n!\Gamma(-n+\alpha+1)}\\
=\frac{(-1)^{-m}(1-x)^{\alpha}(xz+1)^{\beta m+1}}{\beta xz-xz-1}

where
f(x)=x^{m}(1-x)^{\alpha}
and
\phi(x)=(xz+1)^{\beta}
.

Example 3.15
.

(3.15)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n+1}\Gamma(\alpha+1)\left(x(xz+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,-((m+n)\beta);-n+\alpha+1;-z)}{(n+1)!\Gamma(-n+\alpha+1)}\\
=(-1)^{-m}\left(x(xz+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{-m}\left(\frac{x^{m-1}(xz+1)^{\beta-\beta m}\,_{2}F_{1}\left(1,\alpha+(m-1)\beta+2;\alpha+2;\frac{z}{z+1}\right)}{(\alpha+1)(z+1)}\right.\\
\left.+\frac{(x-1)x^{m-1}(1-x)^{\alpha}(xz+1)^{\beta+\beta(m-1)-\beta m+1}\,_{2}F_{1}\left(1,\alpha+(m-1)\beta+2;\alpha+2;\frac{z-xz}{z+1}\right)}{(\alpha+1)(z+1)}\right)

3.8.
Entry 9.

Proposition 3.16
.

(3.16)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(m\beta+n\beta+1)\left(x(x+1)^{-\beta}e^{-xz}\right)^{n}\,_{1}F_{1}(-n;n(\beta-1)+m\beta+1;(-m-n)z)}{\Gamma(n+1)\Gamma(\beta n-n+m\beta+1)}\\
=-\frac{(x+1)\left((x+1)^{\beta}e^{xz}\right)^{m}}{x(\beta+xz+z-1)-1}

where
f(x)=x^{m}
and
\phi(x)=(x+1)^{\beta}e^{xz}
.

Example 3.17
.

(3.17)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(m\beta+n\beta+1)\,_{1}F_{1}(-n;n(\beta-1)+m\beta+1;-((m+n)z))\left(x(x+1)^{-\beta}e^{-xz}\right)^{m+n}}{\Gamma(n+2)\Gamma(\beta n-n+m\beta+1)}\\
=\left(\frac{(x+1)^{\beta}}{x}\right)^{1-m}e^{-((m-1)(x+1)z)}(z-mz)^{\beta+\beta(-m)-1}\Gamma(m\beta-\beta+1,z-mz)\\
-(x+1)^{\beta(m-1)+1}\left(\frac{(x+1)^{\beta}}{x}\right)^{1-m}e^{-((m-1)(x+1)z)}(-((m-1)(x+1)z))^{\beta+\beta(-m)-1}\\
\Gamma(m\beta-\beta+1,-((m-1)(x+1)z))

3.9.
Entry 10.

Proposition 3.18
.

(3.18)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n+1}\left(x(1-x)^{\beta}\right)^{n}\Gamma(1-(m+n)\beta)\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,-\alpha;m-(m+n)(\beta+1)+1;z)}{\Gamma(n+1)\Gamma(-n-(m+n)\beta+1)}\\
=\frac{(-1)^{-m}(x-1)x^{m}\left(x(1-x)^{\beta}\right)^{-m}(1-xz)^{\alpha}}{\beta x+x-1}

where
f(x)=x^{m}(1-xz)^{\alpha}
and
\phi(x)=(1-x)^{-\beta}
.

Example 3.19
.

(3.19)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n+1}\left(x(1-x)^{\beta}\right)^{n}\Gamma(1-(m+n)\beta)\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,-\alpha;m-(m+n)(\beta+1)+1;z)}{\Gamma(n+2)\Gamma(-n-(m+n)\beta+1)}\\
=\frac{(-1)^{-m}(1-x)^{-\beta}}{(\alpha+1)x(z-1)}\left(\,{}_{2}F_{1}\left(1,\alpha-m\beta+\beta+2;\alpha+2;\frac{1}{1-z}\right)\right.\\
\left.-(1-x)^{\beta+\beta(-m)+1}(1-xz)^{\alpha+1}\,_{2}F_{1}\left(1,\alpha-m\beta+\beta+2;\alpha+2;\frac{xz-1}{z-1}\right)\right)

3.10.
Entry 11.

Proposition 3.20
.

(3.20)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(-m-n+\alpha+1)\left(x(x+1)(xz+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{n}}{\Gamma(n+1)\Gamma(m-2(m+n)+\alpha+1)}\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,1-(m+n)\beta;m-2(m+n)+\alpha+1;z)\\
=-\frac{x^{m}(x+1)^{\alpha+1}\left(x(x+1)(xz+1)^{-\beta}\right)^{-m}}{\beta x(x+1)z-(2x+1)(xz+1)}

where
f(x)=\frac{x^{m}(x+1)^{\alpha}}{xz+1}
and
\phi(x)=\frac{(xz+1)^{\beta}}{x+1}
.

Entry 12.

Proposition 3.21
.

(3.21)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(m+n+\alpha+1)\left(\frac{x(xz+1)^{-\beta}}{x+1}\right)^{n}\,_{2}F_{1}(-n,-((m+n)\beta);m+\alpha+1;z)}{\Gamma(n+1)\Gamma(m+\alpha+1)}\\
=-\frac{x^{m}(x+1)^{\alpha+1}(xz+1)\left(\frac{x(xz+1)^{-\beta}}{x+1}\right)^{-m}}{\beta x^{2}z+\beta xz-xz-1}

where
f(x)=x^{m}(x+1)^{\alpha}
and
\phi(x)=(x+1)(xz+1)^{\beta}
.

3.12.
Entry 13.

Proposition 3.22
.

(3.22)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(m+n)(m+n+1)_{m}\Gamma((2m+n)\alpha+1)\left(x(x+1)^{-\alpha}\right)^{2m+n}}{(2m+n)!\Gamma(-m-n+(2m+n)\alpha+2)}\\
\,{}_{3}F_{2}(1,1,-m-n+1;2,2\alpha m-m-n+n\alpha+2;z)\\
=\prod_{p=1}^{\infty}\frac{2(x+1)x^{m+1}}{((1-\alpha)x+1)\left((xz+1)^{2^{-p}}+1\right)}\\
=\frac{(x+1)x^{m}\log(xz+1)}{z((1-\alpha)x+1)}

where
f(x)=x^{m}\log(xz+1)
and
\phi(x)=(x+1)^{\alpha}
and the product is recorded in equation (2.6) in
[
9
]
.

Example 3.23
.

(3.23)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(x(x+1)^{-\alpha}\right)^{n}\Gamma((m+n)\alpha+1)\,_{3}F_{2}(1,1,1-n;2,\alpha n-n+m\alpha+2;z)}{(m+n+1)\Gamma(n)\Gamma(-n+(m+n)\alpha+2)}\\
=\int_{0}^{x}\frac{s^{m}(s+1)^{-\alpha}(x+1)^{\alpha}\left(x(x+1)^{-\alpha}\right)^{-m}\log(sz+1)}{xz}ds

3.13.
Entry 14.

Proposition 3.24
.

(3.24)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{x}{x+1}\right)^{n}\binom{m+n+\alpha}{n-1}\,_{3}F_{2}(1,1,1-n;2,m+\alpha+2;z)\\
=\frac{1}{z}x^{m}\left(\frac{x}{x+1}\right)^{-m}(x+1)^{\alpha+1}\log(xz+1)

where
f(x)=x^{m}(x+1)^{\alpha}\log(xz+1)
and
\phi(x)=x+1
.

Example 3.25
.

(3.25)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(\frac{x}{x+1}\right)^{n}\binom{m+n+\alpha}{n-1}\,_{3}F_{2}(1,1,1-n;2,m+\alpha+2;z)}{m+n+1}\\
=\frac{s^{m}\left(\frac{x}{x+1}\right)^{-m-1}(s+1)^{\alpha-1}\log(sz+1)}{z}

Entry 15.

Proposition 3.26
.

(3.26)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n+1}\Gamma((m+n)\gamma+\delta+1)\left(x(1-x)^{-\gamma}(xz+1)^{-\alpha}\right)^{n}}{\Gamma(n+1)\Gamma(-n+(m+n)\gamma+\delta+1)}\\
\,{}_{2}F_{1}(-n,-((m+n)\alpha)-\beta;m+(m+n)(\gamma-1)+\delta+1;-z)\\
=\frac{(-1)^{-m}x^{m}(1-x)^{\delta+1}(xz+1)^{\beta+1}\left(x(1-x)^{-\gamma}(xz+1)^{-\alpha}\right)^{-m}}{x(\gamma+(\alpha-1)(x-1)z+\gamma xz-1)+1}

where
f(x)=x^{m}(1-x)^{\delta}(xz+1)^{\beta}
and
\phi(x)=(1-x)^{\gamma}(xz+1)^{\alpha}
.

Example 3.27
.

(3.27)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n+1}\Gamma((m+n)\gamma+\delta+1)\left(x(1-x)^{-\gamma}(xz+1)^{-\alpha}\right)^{n}}{(m+n+1)\Gamma(n+1)\Gamma(-n+(m+n)\gamma+\delta+1)}\\
\,{}_{2}F_{1}(-n,-((m+n)\alpha)-\beta;m+(m+n)(\gamma-1)+\delta+1;-z)\\
=\frac{(-1)^{-m}x^{m}(1-x)^{\gamma}(xz+1)^{\alpha}\left(x(1-x)^{-\gamma}(xz+1)^{-\alpha}\right)^{-m}}{m+1}\\
F_{1}(m+1;\gamma-\delta,\alpha-\beta;m+2;x,-xz)

3.15.
Entry 16. The Gegenbauer polynomial

Proposition 3.28
.

(3.28)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(x\left(x^{2}-2\beta x+1\right)^{-\alpha}\right)^{n}C_{n}^{(-((m+n)\alpha))}(\beta)\\
=-\frac{x^{m}\left(x^{2}-2\beta x+1\right)\left(x\left(x^{2}-2\beta x+1\right)^{-\alpha}\right)^{-m}}{2\alpha x^{2}-x^{2}-2\alpha\beta x+2\beta x-1}

where
f(x)=x^{m}
and
\phi(x)=\left(x^{2}-2\beta x+1\right)^{\alpha}
.

Example 3.29
.

(3.29)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(x\left(x^{2}-2\beta x+1\right)^{-\alpha}\right)^{n}C_{n}^{(-m-n)\alpha}(\beta)}{m+n+1}\\
=\frac{\left(\left(\left(\sqrt{\beta^{2}-1}-\beta\right)x+1\right)\left(1-\left(\sqrt{\beta^{2}-1}+\beta\right)x\right)\right)^{\alpha}\left(x^{2}-2\beta x+1\right)^{\alpha m}}{m+1}\\
F_{1}\left(m+1;\alpha,\alpha;m+2;x\left(\beta+\sqrt{\beta^{2}-1}\right),\frac{x}{\beta+\sqrt{\beta^{2}-1}}\right)

3.16.
Entry 17.

Proposition 3.30
.

(3.30)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(x\left(x^{2}-2xz+1\right)^{-\alpha}\right)^{n}C_{n}^{(-((m+n)\alpha)-\beta)}(z)\\
=-\frac{x^{m}\left(x^{2}-2xz+1\right)^{\beta+1}\left(x\left(x^{2}-2xz+1\right)^{-\alpha}\right)^{-m}}{2\alpha x^{2}-x^{2}-2\alpha xz+2xz-1}

where
f(x)=x^{m}\left(x^{2}-2xz+1\right)^{\beta}
and
\phi(x)=\left(x^{2}-2xz+1\right)^{\alpha}
.

Example 3.31
.

(3.31)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(x\left(x^{2}-2xz+1\right)^{-\alpha}\right)^{n}C_{n}^{-(m+n)\alpha-\beta}(z)}{m+n+1}\\
=\frac{x^{m}\left(x^{2}-2xz+1\right)^{\beta}\left(x\left(x^{2}-2xz+1\right)^{-\alpha}\right)^{-m}\left(\frac{x+\sqrt{z^{2}-1}-z}{\sqrt{z^{2}-1}-z}\right)^{\alpha-\beta}\left(\frac{-x+\sqrt{z^{2}-1}+z}{\sqrt{z^{2}-1}+z}\right)^{\alpha-\beta}}{m+1}\\
F_{1}\left(m+1;\alpha-\beta,\alpha-\beta;m+2;\frac{x}{z-\sqrt{z^{2}-1}},\frac{x}{z+\sqrt{z^{2}-1}}\right)

3.17.
Entry 18.

Proposition 3.32
.

(3.32)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left((-1)^{n}+1\right)\left(xe^{-bx^{2}}\right)^{n}(a+b(m+n))^{n/2}}{2\Gamma\left(\frac{n+2}{2}\right)}=\frac{e^{ax^{2}}x^{m}\left(xe^{-bx^{2}}\right)^{-m}}{1-2bx^{2}}

where
f(x)=e^{ax^{2}}x^{m}
and
\phi(x)=e^{bx^{2}}
.

3.18.
Entry 19.

Proposition 3.33
.

(3.33)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{xe^{-bx^{2}}}{x+1}\right)^{n}\,_{2}F_{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{n}{2},-\frac{n}{2};\frac{1}{2},1;a+bn\right)=-\frac{(x+1)e^{ax^{2}}}{2bx^{3}+2bx^{2}-1}

where
f(x)=e^{ax^{2}}x^{m}
and
\phi(x)=(x+1)e^{bx^{2}}
.

Example 3.34
.

(3.34)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(\frac{xe^{-bx^{2}}}{x+1}\right)^{n}\,_{2}F_{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{n}{2},-\frac{n}{2};\frac{1}{2},1;a+bn\right)}{n+1}=\int_{0}^{x}\frac{(x+1)e^{s^{2}(a-b)+bx^{2}}}{(s+1)x}ds

3.19.
Entry 20.

Proposition 3.35
.

(3.35)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n+1}x^{n}\,_{3}F_{2}(1,1,1-n;2,-n+\alpha+2;-z)}{\Gamma(n)\Gamma(-n+\alpha+2)}=\frac{(-1)^{-m}(1-x)^{\alpha}\log(xz+1)}{z\Gamma(\alpha+1)}

where
f(x)=x^{m}(1-x)^{\alpha}\log(xz+1)
and
\phi(x)=1
.

3.20.
Entry 21. The hypergeometric function in terms of infinite double sums

Proposition 3.36
.

(3.36)

\,_{2}F_{1}(a,b;c;x+z)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Gamma(c)x^{n}\Gamma(a+j)\Gamma(b+j)z^{j-n}}{n!\Gamma(a)\Gamma(b)\Gamma(c+j)\Gamma(j-n+1)}

where
f(x)=\,_{2}F_{1}(a,b;c;x+z)
and
\phi(x)=1
.

3.27.
Generalized Abel Theorem

In this section we look at a generalized form of Abel’s theorem where the original form is recorded in the works of Gessel et al.
[
23
]
and Abel
[
11
]
.

Proposition 3.43
.

(3.43)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(xe^{-bx}\right)^{n}(a+bn)^{n-m}}{(n-m)!}=\frac{e^{ax}x^{m}}{1-bx}

where
f(x)=e^{ax}x^{m}
and
\phi(x)=e^{bx}
.

Example 3.44
.

(3.44)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(xe^{-bx}\right)^{n}(a+b(m+n))^{n}}{(n+1)!}=\frac{x^{m-1}e^{-b(m-1)x}\left(xe^{-bx}\right)^{-m}\left(e^{x(a+b(m-1))}-1\right)}{a+b(m-1)}

Example 3.45
.

Generalized Gould form.

(3.45)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(\alpha+\beta(m+n))^{n}(a-\beta(m+n))^{k-m-n}}{n!(k-m-n)!}=\frac{e^{\frac{a+\alpha}{\beta}}\left(\frac{1}{\beta}\right)^{m-k}\Gamma\left(k-m+1,\frac{a+\alpha}{\beta}\right)}{(k-m)!}

Proof.

Use equation (
3.43
) and apply the contour integral method in
[
12
]
. Similar form is recorded in equation (6.10.9) in
[
6
]
.
∎

\sum_{n=-\infty}^{\infty}q^{-n}\log\left(1+e^{2m}q^{2n}\right)\\
=-\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{e^{m}\pi\text{sech}\left(\frac{n\pi^{2}}{\log(q)}\right)\left(\cos\left(\frac{2mn\pi}{\log(q)}\right)\log(q)+2n\pi\sin\left(\frac{2mn\pi}{\log(q)}\right)\right)}{4n^{2}\pi^{2}+\log^{2}(q)}

Example 5.49
.

(5.49)

=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{3/4}e^{-\frac{m}{2}}\pi\text{sech}\left(\frac{n\pi^{2}}{2s}\right)\left(-s\cos\left(\frac{mn\pi}{s}\right)+2n\pi\sin\left(\frac{mn\pi}{s}\right)\right)}{2\left(4n^{2}\pi^{2}+s^{2}\right)}

6.
Definite integrals involving logarithm, quotient Pochammer and q-Pochammer functions

Example 6.1
.

(6.1)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{m}\log^{k}(ax)}{(1+x)_{1+n}}\,dx=\sum_{p=0}^{n}\frac{i(-1)^{p}i^{k}e^{im\pi}(1+p)^{m}(2\pi)^{1+k}\binom{n}{p}\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},-k,\frac{\pi-i\log(a(1+p))}{2\pi}\right)}{n!}

Example 6.2
.

(6.2)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\sqrt{x}\log(\log(ax))}{(1+x)_{1+n}}\,dx=\frac{\pi}{n!}\sum_{p=0}^{n}(-1)^{p}\sqrt{1+p}\binom{n}{p}\log\left(\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{\pi-i\log(a(1+p))}{4\pi}\right)^{2}}{4\pi i\Gamma\left(\frac{3\pi-i\log(a(1+p))}{4\pi}\right)^{2}}\right)

Example 6.3
.

(6.3)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{-m}\left(1-x^{2m}\right)}{\log(x)(1+x)_{1+n}}\,dx\\
=\frac{e^{-im\pi}}{n!}\sum_{p=0}^{n}(-1)^{p}(1+p)^{-m}\binom{n}{p}\left(\Phi\left(e^{-2im\pi},1,\frac{\pi-i\log(1+p)}{2\pi}\right)\right.\\
\left.-e^{2im\pi}(1+p)^{2m}\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},1,\frac{\pi-i\log(1+p)}{2\pi}\right)\right)

Example 6.4
.

(6.4)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\log(\log(ax))}{(1+x)_{1+n}}\,dx=\frac{2\pi}{in!}\sum_{p=0}^{n}(-1)^{p}\binom{n}{p}\log\left(\frac{(a(1+p))^{\frac{\pi-2i\log(2\pi)}{2(2\pi)}}\Gamma\left(\frac{\pi-i\log(a(1+p))}{2\pi}\right)}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\right)

Example 6.5
.

(6.5)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\sqrt[4]{x}\log(\log(ax))}{(1+x)_{1+n}}\,dx=-\frac{\pi}{n!}\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{i}{2}\right)\sum_{p=0}^{n}(-1)^{\frac{1}{4}+p}\sqrt[4]{1+p}\binom{n}{p}\\
\left(\pi-2i\log(2\pi)+(2+2i)\log\left(\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{\pi-i\log(a(1+p))}{8\pi}\right)}{2\Gamma\left(\frac{5}{8}-\frac{i\log(a(1+p))}{8\pi}\right)}\right)\right.\\
\left.-(2-2i)\log\left(\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{3}{8}-\frac{i\log(a(1+p))}{8\pi}\right)}{2\Gamma\left(\frac{7}{8}-\frac{i\log(a(1+p))}{8\pi}\right)}\right)\right)

Example 6.6
.

(6.6)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{m}}{\left(a^{2}+b^{2}-2ia\log(x)-\log^{2}(x)\right)(1+x)_{1+n}}\,dx\\
=\sum_{p=0}^{n}\frac{(-1)^{p}e^{im\pi}(1+p)^{m}\binom{n}{p}\left(\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},1,\frac{a-ib+\pi-i\log(1+p)}{2\pi}\right)-\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},1,\frac{a+ib+\pi-i\log(1+p)}{2\pi}\right)\right)}{2bn!}

Example 6.7
.

(6.7)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{m}\log^{k}(ax)}{(1+bx)_{1+n}}\,dx\\
=-\sum_{p=0}^{n}\frac{(-1)^{1+p}b^{-1-m}e^{im\pi}(1+p)^{m}(2i\pi)^{1+k}\binom{n}{p}\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},-k,\frac{\pi-i\log\left(\frac{a(1+p)}{b}\right)}{2\pi}\right)}{n!}

Example 6.8
.

(6.8)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\log(\log(ax))}{(1+bx)_{1+n}}\,dx\\
=\log\left(\prod_{p=0}^{n}(1+p)^{-\frac{(-1)^{p}\binom{n}{p}\log(2i\pi)}{bn!}}\left(2\pi\Gamma\left(\frac{\pi-i\log\left(\frac{a(1+p)}{b}\right)}{2\pi}\right)\right)^{-\frac{2i(-1)^{p}\pi\binom{n}{p}}{bn!}}\right)

Example 6.9
.

(6.9)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{m}(-x)^{(q)}\log^{k}(ax)}{(1+x)_{1+n}}\,dx\\
=-\sum_{p=0}^{n}\frac{i(-1)^{1+p}i^{k}e^{im\pi}(1+p)^{m}(2\pi)^{1+k}\binom{n}{p}(1+p)^{(q)}\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},-k,\frac{\pi-i\log(a(1+p))}{2\pi}\right)}{n!}

Example 6.10
.

(6.10)

\int_{0}^{\infty}x^{m}\frac{(x)_{q}}{(1+x)_{1+n}}\log^{k}(ax)\,dx\\
=\sum_{p=0}^{n}\frac{i(-1)^{1+p}i^{k}e^{im\pi}(1+p)^{m}(2\pi)^{1+k}\binom{n}{p}(1+p)^{(q)}\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},-k,\frac{\pi-i\log(a(1+p))}{2\pi}\right)}{n!}

Example 6.11
.

(6.11)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{m}(-x)^{(q)}\log^{k}(ax)}{(x+\beta)_{n}}\,dx\\
=-\sum_{p=0}^{n-1}\frac{i(-1)^{1+p}i^{k}e^{im\pi}(2\pi)^{1+k}(p+\beta)^{m}\binom{-1+n}{p}(p+\beta)^{(q)}\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},-k,\frac{\pi-i\log(a(p+\beta))}{2\pi}\right)}{(-1+n)!}

where
n>q

Example 6.12
.

(6.12)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(x^{m}-x^{-m}\right)(x)_{q}}{\log(x)(x+\beta)_{n}}\,dx\\
=\sum_{p=0}^{n}\frac{(-1)^{p}e^{-im\pi}(p+\beta)^{-m}\binom{-1+n}{p}(p+\beta)^{(q)}}{(-1+n)!}\\
\left(\Phi\left(e^{-2im\pi},1,\frac{\pi-i\log(p+\beta)}{2\pi}\right)-e^{2im\pi}(p+\beta)^{2m}\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},1,\frac{\pi-i\log(p+\beta)}{2\pi}\right)\right)

Example 6.13
.

(6.13)

Example 6.14
.

(6.14)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{s}(x;q)_{p}}{(1+b+x)_{1+n}}\,dx=\sum_{j=0}^{p}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\frac{(-1)^{1+j+k}(1+b+k)^{j+s}\pi q^{\frac{1}{2}(-1+j)j}\binom{n}{k}\csc(\pi(j+s))\binom{p}{j}_{q}}{n!}

Example 6.15
.

(6.15)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{m}\log^{k}(ax)(x;q)_{p}}{(1+b+x)_{1+n}}\,dx\\
=\sum_{j=0}^{p}\sum_{l=0}^{n}\frac{(-1)^{j+l}e^{i(j+m)\pi}(1+b+l)^{j+m}(2i\pi)^{1+k}q^{\frac{1}{2}(-1+j)j}\binom{n}{l}}{n!}\\
\Phi\left(e^{2i(j+m)\pi},-k,\frac{\pi-i\log(a(1+b+l))}{2\pi}\right)\binom{p}{j}_{q}

where
n>p

Example 6.16
.

(6.16)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{t}\left(x^{s};q\right){}_{n}}{\left(x^{s};q\right){}_{n+1}}\,dx=\frac{\pi\left(-q^{n}\right)^{-\frac{1+t}{s}}\csc\left(\frac{\pi(1+t)}{s}\right)}{s}

Example 6.17
.

(6.17)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{m}\log^{k}(ax)\left(x^{\alpha};q\right){}_{n}}{\left(x^{\alpha};q\right){}_{1+n}}\,dx\\
=\frac{e^{\frac{i(1+m)\pi}{\alpha}}(2\pi)^{1+k}\left(-q^{n}\right)^{-\frac{1+m}{\alpha}}\left(\frac{i}{\alpha}\right)^{-1+k}\Phi\left(e^{\frac{2i(1+m)\pi}{\alpha}},-k,\frac{\pi-i\alpha\log\left(a\left(-q^{n}\right)^{-1/\alpha}\right)}{2\pi}\right)}{\alpha^{2}}

Example 6.18
.

(6.18)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{-1-m+r}\log^{k}\left(\frac{a}{x}\right)}{\left(1+bx^{r}\right)^{n}}\,dx\\
=\sum_{j=0}^{n-1}\sum_{l=0}^{j}\frac{(-1)^{-j+n}b^{-1+\frac{m}{r}}e^{\frac{im\pi}{r}}m^{l}(2i\pi)^{1-j+k+l}r^{-1-k-l}\binom{j}{l}\Gamma(1+k)}{\Gamma(1-j+k+l)\Gamma(n)}\\
\Phi\left(e^{\frac{2im\pi}{r}},j-k-l,\frac{\pi-ir\log\left(ab^{1/r}\right)}{2\pi}\right)S_{-1+n}^{(j)}

Example 6.19
.

(6.19)

\int_{0}^{1}(1-x)^{m}\left(\frac{x}{1-x}\right)^{p}\,dx\\
=\sum_{j=0}^{m}\sum_{q=0}^{j}\frac{(-1)^{m}(-m+p)\pi\csc(p\pi)(-1)^{m-j}S_{m}^{(j)}\binom{j}{q}(1-m)^{j-q}p^{q}}{(1+m)!}

Example 6.20
.

(6.20)

\int_{0}^{1}(1-x)^{r}\left(\frac{x}{1-x}\right)^{m}\log^{k}\left(\frac{ax}{1-x}\right)\,dx\\
=\sum_{j=0}^{r}\sum_{q=0}^{j}\frac{k!(-1)^{-j+2r}e^{im\pi}(2i\pi)^{k-q}(1+m-r)^{j-q}\binom{j}{q}}{\Gamma(1+k-q)\Gamma(2+r)}\\
\left(-2i\pi(m-r)\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},-k+q,\frac{\pi-i\log(a)}{2\pi}\right)\right.\\
\left.+(-k+q)\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},1-k+q,\frac{\pi-i\log(a)}{2\pi}\right)\right)S_{r}^{(j)}

Example 6.21
.

(6.21)

\int_{0}^{1}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{p}q^{\frac{1}{2}(-1+k)k}x^{n}\left(-1+x^{-1-n}\right)^{kw}\binom{p}{k}_{q}\right)\,dx=\sum_{k=1}^{p}\frac{k\pi q^{\frac{1}{2}(-1+k)k}w\csc(k\pi w)\binom{p}{k}_{q}}{1+n}

Example 6.22
.

(6.22)

\int_{0}^{1}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p}q^{\frac{1}{2}(-1+j)j}x^{n}\left(-1+x^{-1-n}\right)^{jm}\log^{k}\left(a\left(-1+x^{-1-n}\right)^{j}\right)\binom{p}{j}_{q}\right)\,dx\\
=-\sum_{j=1}^{p}\frac{e^{ijm\pi}(ij)^{k}(2\pi)^{k}q^{\frac{1}{2}(-1+j)j}}{1+n}\\
\left(k\Phi\left(e^{2ijm\pi},1-k,\frac{1}{2}-\frac{i\log(a)}{2j\pi}\right)+2ijm\pi\Phi\left(e^{2ijm\pi},-k,\frac{1}{2}-\frac{i\log(a)}{2j\pi}\right)\right)\binom{p}{j}_{q}

(6.23)

=\sum_{k=0}^{p}\frac{(1+k)\pi q^{\frac{1}{2}(-1+k)k}w\csc((1+k)\pi w)\binom{p}{k}_{q}}{1+n}

7.
The Polylogarithm function

In this section we develop a table of generating functions involving Hurwitz-Lerch zeta function in terms of definite integrals of the polylogarithm function at low-order. In this section we used the binomial coefficient
\binom{n}{j}
, Pochhammer symbol
(n)_{j}
. We used Eq. (2.5.2.1) in
[
15
]
and the method in
[
12
]
.

Example 7.1
.

(7.1)

\int_{0}^{\infty}x^{-1+m}\log^{k}(ax)\text{Li}_{n}(-cx)\,dx\\
=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{1-n}i^{1+j}c^{-m}e^{\frac{1}{2}i(k+2m)\pi}m^{-j-n}(2\pi)^{1-j+k}\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},j-k,\frac{\pi-i\log\left(\frac{a}{c}\right)}{2\pi}\right)(1-j+k)_{j}(n)_{j}}{\Gamma(1+j)}

Example 7.2
.

(7.2)

\int_{0}^{\infty}x^{-1+m}\log^{k}(-cx)\text{Li}_{n}(-cx)\,dx\\
=-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{c^{-m}e^{-im\pi}(-m)^{-j-n}(2i\pi)^{1-j+k}\binom{-1+j+n}{j}k!\text{Li}_{j-k}\left(e^{2im\pi}\right)}{(-j+k)!}

Example 7.3
.

(7.3)

\int_{0}^{\infty}x^{-1+m}\log^{k}(-cx)\text{Li}_{n}(-cx)\,dx\\
=-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}c^{-m}e^{-i(j+m)\pi}(-m)^{-j-n}\binom{-1+j+n}{j}\Gamma(1+k)\\
\left(i^{2j}\zeta(1-j+k,-m)-i^{2k}\zeta(1-j+k,1+m)\right)

Example 7.4
.

(7.4)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\log^{k}(ax)\text{Li}_{n}(-cx)}{\sqrt{x}}\,dx\\
=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{-j-n}i^{-j+k}2^{1-j+2k+n}\pi^{1-j+k}\binom{-1+j+n}{j}k!}{\sqrt{c}(-j+k)!}\\
\left(\zeta\left(j-k,\frac{\pi-i\log\left(\frac{a}{c}\right)}{4\pi}\right)-\zeta\left(j-k,\frac{3}{4}-\frac{i\log\left(\frac{a}{c}\right)}{4\pi}\right)\right)

Example 7.5
.

(7.5)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\log^{k}(ax)\text{Li}_{n}(-cx)}{x^{3/2}}\,dx\\
=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{i^{-j+k}2^{1-j+2k+n}\sqrt{c}\pi^{1-j+k}\binom{-1+j+n}{j}k!\left(-\zeta\left(j-k,\frac{\pi-i\log\left(\frac{a}{c}\right)}{4\pi}\right)+\zeta\left(j-k,\frac{3}{4}-\frac{i\log\left(\frac{a}{c}\right)}{4\pi}\right)\right)}{(-j+k)!}

Example 7.6
.

(7.6)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\log^{k}(-cx)\text{Li}_{n}(-cx)}{\sqrt{x}}\,dx\\
=-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{i(-1)^{-j-n}2^{1+k+n}\left(1-2^{1-j+k}\right)(i\pi)^{1-j+k}\binom{-1+j+n}{j}k!\zeta(j-k)}{\sqrt{c}(-j+k)!}

Example 7.7
.

(7.7)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\log^{k}(-cx)\text{Li}_{n}(-cx)}{x^{3/2}}\,dx\\
=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{i^{2-j+k}2^{1+k+n}\left(1-2^{1-j+k}\right)\sqrt{c}\pi^{1-j+k}\binom{-1+j+n}{j}k!\zeta(j-k)}{(-j+k)!}

8.
Logarithmic function generating functions

Example 8.1
.

(8.1)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{m}\sqrt{\log(x)}\log(\log(x))}{1+cx}\,dx\\
=-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{i^{j}2^{\frac{1}{2}-j}c^{-1-m}e^{\frac{1}{4}i(1+4m)\pi}m^{-j}\pi^{\frac{3}{2}-j}(0)_{j}\left(\frac{3}{2}-j\right)_{j}}{\Gamma(1+j)}\\
\left(\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},-\frac{1}{2}+j,\frac{\pi-i\log\left(\frac{1}{c}\right)}{2\pi}\right)\left(\pi+2iH_{\frac{1}{2}-j}+i(-4+\log(4)-2\log(\pi))\right)\right.\\
\left.+2i\Phi^{\prime}\left(e^{2im\pi},-\frac{1}{2}+j,\frac{\pi-i\log\left(\frac{1}{c}\right)}{2\pi}\right)\right)

where
Re(m)<0

Example 8.2
.

(8.2)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{m}\log(\log(x))}{(1+cx)\sqrt{\log(x)}}\,dx\\
=-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{i^{j}2^{-\frac{1}{2}-j}c^{-1-m}e^{\frac{1}{4}i(-1+4m)\pi}m^{-j}\pi^{\frac{1}{2}-j}(0)_{j}\left(\frac{1}{2}-j\right)_{j}}{\Gamma(1+j)}\\
\left(\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},\frac{1}{2}+j,\frac{\pi-i\log\left(\frac{1}{c}\right)}{2\pi}\right)\left(\pi+2iH_{-\frac{1}{2}-j}+2i\log\left(\frac{2}{\pi}\right)\right)\right.\\
\left.+2i\Phi^{\prime}\left(e^{2im\pi},\frac{1}{2}+j,\frac{\pi-i\log\left(\frac{1}{c}\right)}{2\pi}\right)\right)

Example 8.3
.

(8.3)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{m}\log^{k}(x)}{1-c^{2}x^{2}}\,dx\\
=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{i^{1+j}2^{-j+k}(-c)^{-m}c^{-1-m}e^{\frac{1}{2}i(k+2m)\pi}m^{-j}\pi^{1-j+k}}{\Gamma(1+j)}\\
\left(-c^{m}\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},j-k,\frac{\pi-i\log\left(-\frac{1}{c}\right)}{2\pi}\right)+(-c)^{m}\Phi\left(e^{2im\pi},j-k,\frac{\pi-i\log\left(\frac{1}{c}\right)}{2\pi}\right)\right)\\
(0)_{j}(1-j+k)_{j}

where
Im(c)\neq 0

Example 8.4
.

(8.4)

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\sum_{q=0}^{2p+1}\frac{(-1)^{j}e^{m+2jm}m^{1+2p-q}\binom{1+2p}{q}\log^{k-q}\left(ae^{1+2j}\right)}{(1+2p)!(k-q)!}\\
=\frac{2^{1+k}e^{2m}\Phi\left(-e^{2m},-k,1+\frac{\log(a)}{2}\right)-\log^{k}(a)}{2k!}

9.
A few definite integrals

Example 9.1
.

(9.1)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\log\left(\frac{(1+x)\left(b^{2}+x\right)}{(b+x)^{2}}\right)(2+\log(ax)\log(\log(ax)))}{2\sqrt{x}\log(ax)}\,dx\\
=\frac{1}{4}\pi\left(2i\left(-1+\sqrt{b}\right)^{2}\pi+24(1+b)\log(2)+4\left(-1+\sqrt{b}\right)^{2}\log(\pi)-8\log(-3\pi-i\log(a))\right.\\
\left.+8\log\left(\frac{1}{4}(-\pi-i\log(a))\right)+16\sqrt{b}(\log(-3\pi-i\log(a)-i\log(b))\right.\\
\left.-\log(-2(\pi+i\log(a)+i\log(b))))-8b\log(-3\pi-i\log(a)-2i\log(b))\right.\\
\left.+8b\log\left(\frac{1}{4}(-\pi-i\log(a)-2i\log(b))\right)+8\text{log$\Gamma$}\left(-\frac{\pi+i\log(a)}{4\pi}\right)\right.\\
\left.-8\left(\text{log$\Gamma$}\left(-\frac{3}{4}-\frac{i\log(a)}{4\pi}\right)+2\sqrt{b}\text{log$\Gamma$}\left(-\frac{\pi+i\log(a)+i\log(b)}{4\pi}\right)\right.\right.\\
\left.\left.-2\sqrt{b}\text{log$\Gamma$}\left(-\frac{3\pi+i\log(a)+i\log(b)}{4\pi}\right)-b\text{log$\Gamma$}\left(-\frac{\pi+i\log(a)+2i\log(b)}{4\pi}\right)\right.\right.\\
\left.\left.+b\text{log$\Gamma$}\left(-\frac{3\pi+i\log(a)+2i\log(b)}{4\pi}\right)\right)\right)

(9.2)

Example 9.12

(9.12)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\log\left(\frac{1+(1+x)^{2}}{1+x^{2}}\right)(2+\log(-x)\log(\log(-x)))}{\sqrt{x}\log(-x)}\,dx\\

+\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{i(-1)^{n}e^{-i\left(2i+n^{2}\pi^{2}\right)}\left(-\Gamma\left(0,-i\left(2i+n^{2}\pi^{2}\right)\right)+e^{2i\left(2i+n^{2}\pi^{2}\right)}\Gamma\left(0,i\left(2i+n^{2}\pi^{2}\right)\right)\right)}{2i+n^{2}\pi^{2}}

Example 11.1

From Eq. (7.704) in
[
31
]

(11.1)

\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(-1)^{k}a^{-m+t^{-\beta}}(1-t)^{-1+\gamma}t^{-1+\alpha+k\beta}\Gamma\left(1+k,\left(-m+t^{-\beta}\right)\log(a)\right)}{\left(-1+mt^{\beta}\right)^{k+1}}\,dt\\
=-\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=0}^{n}\frac{m^{-j+n}(1-j+k)_{j}\binom{n}{j}(-1+\gamma)!\log^{-j+k}(a)}{(\alpha+n\beta)(1+\alpha+n\beta)_{-1+\gamma}}

\left((-1)^{n}+(-1)^{m+n}+2\csc\left(\frac{n\pi}{m}\right)\sin\left(\frac{\pi\left(n+2n\left\lfloor\frac{1}{2}(-1+m)\right\rfloor\right)}{m}\right)\right)

Example 11.121
.

(11.121)

\left(-1+ae^{x}\right)^{m}=(-1+a)^{m}+(-1)^{m+1}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{ax^{n}(-1)^{1+j}a^{-1+j}j^{n}\binom{m}{j}}{n!}

where
|Re(a)|<1,|Re(x)|<1,m\in\mathbb{Z_{+}}
.

12.
A short note on a definite integral.

Example 12.1
.

(12.1)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\log^{k}(ax)x^{-1+m}(1+cx)^{u}}{\left(1+bx^{n}\right)^{p}}\,dx\\
=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{r=0}^{p-1}\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\frac{i(-1)^{p}i^{k-l}b^{-\frac{j+m}{n}}e^{\frac{i(j+m)\pi}{n}}c^{j}(j+m-n)^{-l+r}n^{-1-k+l-r}(2\pi)^{1+k-l}\binom{r}{l}\binom{u}{j}}{\Gamma(p)}\\
\times\Phi\left(e^{\frac{2i(j+m)\pi}{n}},-k+l,\frac{\pi-in\left(\log(a)+\log\left(b^{-1/n}\right)\right)}{2\pi}\right)(1+k-l)_{l}S_{-1+p}^{(r)}

where
Re(a)<0,Re(m)>0
.

Example 12.2
.

(12.2)

B(m,p)=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{r=0}^{p-1}\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\frac{i(-1)^{p}i^{-l}e^{i(j+m)\pi}(-1+j+m)^{-l+r}(2\pi)^{1-l}\binom{-m}{j}\binom{r}{l}}{\Gamma(p)}\\
\times\Phi\left(e^{2i(j+m)\pi},l,\frac{1}{2}\right)(1-l)_{l}S_{-1+p}^{(r)}

where
Re(m)<-\pi
.

13.
A table of formulae involving the
arcsin(x)
function and special functions.

In this section we derive definite integrals and series involving the arcsin function in terms of special functions. In this work we have taken a combinatorial approach to deriving the
arcsin
function in terms of the hypergeometric function. This process yielded a new special function forms for this trigonometric function not seen in current literature to the best of our knowledge. This form allowed us to write down definite integrals involving elliptic functions and Catalan’s constant. The
arcsin
function is used in the special integral Owen T-function
[
34
]
, pp.137. when dealing with wireless communications theory.

Lemma 13.1
.

(13.1)

\sinh^{-1}(x)=-\frac{x^{3}\,_{2}F_{1}\left(\frac{3}{4},\frac{3}{4};\frac{7}{4};\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\sqrt{1-x^{4}}\right)\right)}{3\sqrt[4]{2}\left(1+\sqrt{1-x^{4}}\right)^{3/4}}+\frac{\sqrt[4]{2}x\,_{2}F_{1}\left(\frac{1}{4},\frac{1}{4};\frac{5}{4};\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{4}}}{2}\right)}{\sqrt[4]{1+\sqrt{1-x^{4}}}}

Use (
1.1
) where
f(x)=\log\left(\sqrt{x^{2}+1}+x\right)
and
\phi(x)=1
.

=\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\frac{(-1)^{k}\left(x-\cos\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)\right)\csc\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)\left(1+\left(\cot\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)-x\csc\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{2}\right)^{-3/2}}{3\times 2^{3/4}\left(1+\sqrt{1-\frac{\left(x-\cos\left(\frac{(1+2k)\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{4}\csc^{4}\left(\frac{(1+2k)\pi}{2n}\right)}{\left(1+\left(x-\cos\left(\frac{(1+2k)\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{2}\csc^{2}\left(\frac{(1+2k)\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{2}}}\right)^{3/4}}\\
\left(\sqrt{2}\left(\cot\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)-x\csc\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{2}\right.\\
\left.\,{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\frac{3}{4},\frac{3}{4};\frac{7}{4};\frac{1}{8}\left(4-2\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{\left(3+4x^{2}-8x\cos\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)+\cos\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{n}\right)\right)\sin^{2}\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)}{\left(1+x^{2}-2x\cos\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{2}}}\right)\right)\right.\\
\left.+6\sqrt{1+\sqrt{1-\frac{\left(x-\cos\left(\frac{(1+2k)\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{4}\csc^{4}\left(\frac{(1+2k)\pi}{2n}\right)}{\left(1+\left(x-\cos\left(\frac{(1+2k)\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{2}\csc^{2}\left(\frac{(1+2k)\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{2}}}}\right.\\
\left.\left(1+\left(\cot\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)-x\csc\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{2}\right)\right.\\
\left.\,{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\frac{1}{4},\frac{1}{4};\frac{5}{4};\frac{1}{8}\left(4-2\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{\left(3+4x^{2}-8x\cos\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)+\cos\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{n}\right)\right)\sin^{2}\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)}{\left(1+x^{2}-2x\cos\left(\frac{\pi+2k\pi}{2n}\right)\right)^{2}}}\right)\right)\right)

(14.48)

\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{k}J_{\frac{k}{2}}(kz){}^{2}=-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{1-4z^{2}}}-\frac{\sin^{-1}(2z)}{\pi\sqrt{1-4z^{2}}}\\
